# Public API changes since v0.21.1

The complete list of what changed in the public API between the published `documonster@0.21.1` / `@documonster/mcp@0.21.1` and the current tree. [MIGRATION.md](../MIGRATION.md#unreleased) says what to do about it; this file says, name by name, what changed.

## How to use this list

- **Looking for an old name?** Search this page for it. A deprecated name's row gives its replacement; a name under "Changed signature or type" shows the old and new signatures side by side.
- **Looking for one module?** Follow its link in the summary below. Each entry has the same sections, in this order: removed, became optional, changed, deprecated, deprecated call forms, added to existing, added.
- **Want to know what to do, not what changed?** Read [MIGRATION.md](../MIGRATION.md#the-most-common-changes-before-and-after) instead; this list does not describe behaviour that changed without a change of type.

## How this list is made

It is generated by `pnpm api:changes` (`scripts/api-changes.ts`), not written. The declarations of every `exports` subpath are read with the TypeScript compiler — the published package for 0.21.1, a fresh build for the current tree — once with the default conditions and once with `browser`. Every public name is recorded: each namespace member (recursively), each class's constructor, instance and static members, each overload of a function, each field of an interface or object type, each enum member and constant. Private and protected members are not public and are skipped. The two records are then compared name by name.

- **Signatures are shown as the compiler prints them**, one overload per line. A line longer than 160 characters is clipped to the stretch that differs from the signature it is compared with, marked `…` where cut, and at most four overloads are shown per cell (the rest are counted). The whole signature is one hover away in your editor, or in the package's `.d.ts` files.
- A change is reported only when the printed type differs after removing differences that are only printing: a type printed under its new name (when the old name is now a deprecated alias of it), an internal import alias (`…Impl`), and the order of a union of string literals.
- An interface that became an alias of a renamed interface (`type Old = New`) is compared field by field, so a field that changed shows up as its own row.
- `[browser]` sections list only what differs from the Node entry of the same subpath.
- `Name#member` is an instance member; `Name.member` is a namespace member, a static member or a field.
- Members that every `Error` has (`captureStackTrace`, `stackTraceLimit`, …) are left out.
- Behaviour that changed without a change of type is not visible here; MIGRATION.md describes it.

| `toNodeReadable` | function | Use `Archive.toNodeReadable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `TarGzArchive` | class | Use `Archive.TarGzArchive` (or build one with `Archive.createTarGz`). Removed in 1.0. |
| `TarGzArchive#bytes` | method | Use `toBuffer`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `TarGzArchive#bytesSync` | method | Use `toBufferSync`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `targz` | function | Use `Archive.packTarGz`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `parseTarGz` | function | Use `Archive.parseTarGz`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `parseTarGzStream` | function | Use `Archive.parseTarGzStream`, which decompresses and parses together in bounded memory; this one decompresses the whole archive before the first entry. For that all-or-nothing behaviour iterate `Archive.parseTarGz`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `untargz` | function | Use `Archive.unpackTarGz`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ParseTarGzOptions.maxFileSize?` | prop | Use `maxEntrySize`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.createAbortError` | function | Use `new AbortError(reason)` (a flat export of `documonster/archive` and `documonster/stream`); this one returns `reason` itself when it already is an `AbortError`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.isAbortError` | function | Use the flat `isAbortError` export. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.zip` | function | Use `createZip`, or `createTar` for `{ format: "tar" }`. `zip` named a builder while `tar` named bytes. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.unzip` | function | Use `openZip`, or `openTar` for `{ format: "tar" }`. `unzip` named a reader while `untar` named a map. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.ZipArchive#bytes` | method | Use `toBuffer`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.ZipArchive#bytesSync` | method | Use `toBufferSync`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.ZipEditor#getEntries` | method | Use `getSourceEntries`. The name read as the archive the edits produce — which `ArchiveFile#getEntries` returns — while this has always returned the source's entries. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.ZipEditor#bytes` | method | Use `toBuffer`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.editZip` | function | Use `ZipEditor.open`; this only forwards to it. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.editZipUrl` | function | Use `ZipEditor.openUrl`; this only forwards to it. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.ZipEditPlan#toJSON` | method | Use `toJson`. Being named `toJSON`, this is also what `JSON.stringify(plan)` calls — and since it returns a Promise, that call serialises `{}`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.ZipEditPlan.fromJSON` | static | Use `ZipEditPlan.fromJson`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.UnzipEntry` | class | Entries come from a reader — `ZipReader.entries()` / `get()`, or iterating `Archive.openZip(…)` — and the shapes this takes are the reader's own. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.TAR_TYPE` | const | Use `TarType`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.TarArchive#bytes` | method | Use `toBuffer`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.TarArchive#bytesSync` | method | Use `toBufferSync`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.createTarArchive` | function | Use `createTar`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.createTarReader` | function | Use `openTar`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.tar` | function | Use `packTar`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.tarSync` | function | Use `packTarSync`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.untar` | function | Use `unpackTar`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.base64ToUint8Array` | function | Not an archive operation. Decode natively: `Uint8Array.fromBase64(s)` where the runtime has it (not Node 22), `Buffer.from(s, "base64")` on Node, or `Uint8Array.from(atob(s), c => c.charCodeAt(0))` in a browser. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.uint8ArrayToBase64` | function | Not an archive operation. Encode natively: `bytes.toBase64()` where the runtime has it (not Node 22), `Buffer.from(bytes).toString("base64")` on Node, or `btoa(Array.from(bytes, b => String.fromCharCode(b)).join(""))` in a browser. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.concatUint8Arrays` | function | Not an archive operation. Natively: `const out = new Uint8Array(total)`, then `out.set(part, offset)` for each part (or `Buffer.concat(parts)` on Node). Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.stringToUint8Array` | function | Not an archive operation. Use `new TextEncoder().encode(str)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.uint8ArrayToString` | function | Not an archive operation. Use `new TextDecoder(encoding).decode(bytes)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.ZIP_CRYPTO_HEADER_SIZE` | const | Use `Crypto.ZIP_CRYPTO_HEADER_SIZE`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.zipCryptoInitKeys` | function | Use `Crypto.createZipCryptoKeys`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.zipCryptoDecrypt` | function | Use `Crypto.decryptZipCrypto`, which throws an `ArchiveError` where this returns `null`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.zipCryptoEncrypt` | function | Use `Crypto.encryptZipCrypto`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Archive.AES_VENDOR_ID` | const | Use `Crypto.AES_VENDOR_ID`. Removed in 1.0. |

| Name | Kind | Signature |
| --- | --- | --- |

### Newly deprecated (still works; removed in 1.0)

| Name | Kind | Replacement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `buildChartModel` | function | Use `ChartClassic.createModel`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `buildComboChartModel` | function | Use `ChartClassic.createComboModel`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `fillChartCaches` | function | Use `ChartClassic.fillCaches(model, workbook, { worksheet })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `fillChartExCaches` | function | Use `ChartEx.fillCaches(model, workbook, { worksheet })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `fillNumRef` | function | Use `ChartClassic.fillNumRef(ref, workbook, { date1904, worksheet })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `fillStrRef` | function | Use `ChartClassic.fillStrRef(ref, workbook, { worksheet })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `parseChartColors` | function | Use `ChartClassic.fromColorsXml`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `buildChartColors` | function | Use `ChartClassic.toColorsXml`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `parseChartStyle` | function | Use `ChartClassic.fromStyleXml`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `buildChartStyle` | function | Use `ChartClassic.toStyleXml`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `buildChartExModel` | function | Use `ChartEx.createModel`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `parseChartEx` | function | Use `ChartEx.fromXml`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `canRenderChartExAsVectorPdf` | function | Every Office 2016 chart has a vector picture: `ChartEx.toDrawList(model)` draws any model, and `Pdf.fromChart` picks its path itself. This still says whether each series' layout is one of `VECTOR_PDF_CHART_EX_LAYOUT_IDS`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `drawChartExPdf` | function | Put the chart on the page as a display list, as every producer does: `Draw.render(ChartEx.toDrawList(model, { width, height }), Pdf.createDrawSurface(page, rect))`. This draws that same display list — the chart and its chart area — onto a surface with `drawRect` / `drawLine` / `drawText`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `renderChartExPng` | function | Use `ChartEx.toPng`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `renderChartExSvg` | function | Use `ChartEx.toSvg`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `VECTOR_PDF_CHART_EX_LAYOUT_IDS` | const | It only answers the deprecated `canRenderChartExAsVectorPdf`: every Office 2016 chart now has a vector picture through `ChartEx.toDrawList`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `renderChartEx` | function | Use `ChartEx.toXml` — this returns the part's XML, not a picture. Removed in 1.0. |
| `applyChartExPreset` | function | Use `ChartEx.applyPreset`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `applyChartPreset` | function | Use `ChartClassic.applyPreset`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `EXCEL_CHART_EX_PRESETS` | const | The preset names are the keys of the table: `Object.keys(CHART_EX_PRESETS)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `EXCEL_CHART_PRESETS` | const | The preset names are the keys of the table: `Object.keys(CHART_PRESETS)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `chartExOptionsFromRows` | function | Use `ChartEx.createOptionsFromRows`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `chartExOptionsFromTable` | function | Use `ChartEx.createOptionsFromTable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `chartOptionsFromRows` | function | Use `ChartClassic.createOptionsFromRows`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `chartOptionsFromTable` | function | Use `ChartClassic.createOptionsFromTable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `seriesFromColumns` | function | Use `ChartClassic.createSeriesFromColumns`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `applyAxisTransform` | function | Use `ChartClassic.Backend.applyAxisTransform`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `buildChartScene` | function | Use `ChartClassic.Backend.toScene`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `buildEffectFilter` | function | Use `ChartClassic.Backend.toSvgFilter`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `drawChartPdf` | function | Put the chart on the page as a display list, as every producer does: `Draw.render(ChartClassic.toDrawList(model, { width, height }), Pdf.createDrawSurface(page, rect))`. This draws that same display list — the chart and its chart area — onto a surface with `drawRect` / `drawLine` / `drawText`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `renderChartPng` | function | Use `ChartClassic.toPng`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `renderChartSvg` | function | Use `ChartClassic.toSvg`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ChartPdfDrawingSurface` | type | A chart goes onto a PDF page as a display list, through `Pdf.createDrawSurface` (a `PdfDrawPage`). Removed in 1.0. |
| `ChartPdfPathOp` | type | A path operation of `ChartPdfDrawingSurface`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ChartRenderOptions.backgroundColor?` | prop | Use `background`, which also takes `{ r, g, b, a }`. When both are given, `background` wins. Removed in 1.0. |
| `PdfChartRenderOptions` | type | Pass `ChartRenderOptions` to `ChartClassic.toDrawList` and the box to `Pdf.createDrawSurface`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `PdfChartRenderOptions.backgroundColor?` | prop | Use `background`, which also takes `{ r, g, b, a }`. When both are given, `background` wins. Removed in 1.0. |
| `PdfColor` | type | See `ChartPdfColor`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `resolveTopologyObject` | function | Use `ChartClassic.Backend.resolveTopologyObject`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `parseSpPr` | function | Use `DrawingMl.parseSpPr`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `parseTxPr` | function | Use `DrawingMl.parseTxPr`. Removed in 1.0. |

### Newly deprecated (still works; removed in 1.0)

| Name | Kind | Replacement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `HeaderArray` | type | Use `CsvHeaderArray`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RowHashArray` | type | Use `CsvRowHashArray`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RowArray` | type | Use `CsvRowArray`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RowMap` | type | Use `CsvRowMap`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Row` | type | Use `CsvRow`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `HeaderTransformFunction` | type | Use `CsvHeaderTransformFunction`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RowTransformCallback` | type | Use `CsvRowTransformCallback`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RowTransformFunction` | type | Use `CsvRowTransformFunction`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RowValidateCallback` | type | Use `CsvRowValidateCallback`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RowValidateFunction` | type | Use `CsvRowValidateFunction`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `TransformContext` | type | Use `CsvTransformContext`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `FormattedValue` | type | Use `CsvFormattedValue`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `TransformResult` | type | Use `CsvTransformResult`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `TypeTransformMap` | type | Use `CsvTypeTransformMap`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `DynamicTypingConfig` | type | Use `CsvDynamicTypingConfig`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `CastDateConfig` | type | Use `CsvCastDateConfig`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ColumnConfig` | type | Use `CsvColumnConfig`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `OnSkipCallback` | type | Use `CsvOnSkipCallback`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `CsvParseOptions.skipRecordsWithError?` | prop | Has no effect: a malformed record (a column-count mismatch the `columnMismatch` strategy rejects, or text after a closing quote) is always left out of `rows`, reported in `errors` / `invalidRows` and handed to `onSkip`. It only used to decide whether `onSkip` was called. Removed in 1.0. |
| `CsvParseArrayOptions.skipRecordsWithError?` | prop | Has no effect: a malformed record (a column-count mismatch the `columnMismatch` strategy rejects, or text after a closing quote) is always left out of `rows`, reported in `errors` / `invalidRows` and handed to `onSkip`. It only used to decide whether `onSkip` was called. Removed in 1.0. |
| `CsvParseObjectOptions.skipRecordsWithError?` | prop | Has no effect: a malformed record (a column-count mismatch the `columnMismatch` strategy rejects, or text after a closing quote) is always left out of `rows`, reported in `errors` / `invalidRows` and handed to `onSkip`. It only used to decide whether `onSkip` was called. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ChunkMeta` | type | Use `CsvChunkMeta`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RecordInfo` | type | Use `CsvRecordInfo`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RecordInfo.invalid_field_length?` | prop | Never set by any parser. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RecordWithInfo` | type | Use `CsvRecordWithInfo`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ColumnMismatchConfig` | type | Use `CsvColumnMismatchConfig`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ColumnMismatchLess` | type | Use `CsvColumnMismatchLess`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ColumnMismatchMore` | type | Use `CsvColumnMismatchMore`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.quoted` | function | Use `forceQuote`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.unquoted` | function | Use `preventQuote`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.parseRows` | function | Use `parseStream`. An incremental reader takes the `Stream` suffix, as `Xml.parseSaxStream` and `Workbook.readStream` do. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.parseWithProgress` | function | Use `parseAsync` with `{ onProgress }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.createParserStream` | function | Use `new Csv.ParserStream(options)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.createFormatterStream` | function | Use `new Csv.FormatterStream(options)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.rowHashArrayToValues` | function | Use `getRowValues`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.rowHashArrayToHeaders` | function | Use `getRowHeaders`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.rowHashArrayMapByHeaders` | function | Use `getRowValuesByHeaders`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.generateAsync` | function | Use `generateRowsAsync`. `Async` marks the asynchronous sibling of a synchronous form, so this read as the sibling of `generate`, which returns the whole CSV; what it does is yield rows, as `generateRows` does. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ColumnDef` | type | Use `CsvColumnDef`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `GeneratorColumnConfig` | type | Use `CsvGeneratorColumnConfig`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `BuiltinColumnType` | type | Use `CsvBuiltinColumnType`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `GeneratorFn` | type | Use `CsvGeneratorFn`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `GeneratorContext` | type | Use `CsvGeneratorContext`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `StopCondition` | type | Use `CsvStopCondition`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `StopContext` | type | Use `CsvStopContext`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `DecimalSeparator` | type | Use `CsvDecimalSeparator`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ParsedRow` | type | Use `CsvParsedRow`. Removed in 1.0. |

### Newly deprecated call forms (the name stays; the form described is removed in 1.0)

| Name | Replacement |
| --- | --- |
| `Csv.detectDelimiter` | Pass `{ quote, delimitersToGuess, comment }`. The positional form is removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.detectLinebreak` | Pass `{ quote }`. The positional form is removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.applyDynamicTypingToRow` | Pass `castDate` as `{ castDate }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.formatNumber` | Pass the separator as `{ decimalSeparator }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Csv.parseNumber` | Pass the separator as `{ decimalSeparator }`. Removed in 1.0. |

| Name | Kind | Signature |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `CsvParseOptions.decimalSeparator?` | prop | `"." \| "," \| undefined` |
| `CsvParseArrayOptions.decimalSeparator?` | prop | `"." \| "," \| undefined` |
| `CsvParseObjectOptions.decimalSeparator?` | prop | `"." \| "," \| undefined` |
| `Csv.forceQuote` | function | `(value: string): FormattedValue` |
| `Csv.preventQuote` | function | `(value: string): FormattedValue` |
| `Csv.parseStream` | function | `(input: CsvAsyncInput, options?: CsvParseOptions \| undefined): AsyncGenerator<ParsedRow, void, unknown>` |
| `Csv.getRowValues` | function | `<V = unknown>(row: RowHashArray<V>): V[]` |
| `Csv.getRowHeaders` | function | `(row: RowHashArray): string[]` |
| `Csv.getRowValuesByHeaders` | function | `<V = unknown>(row: RowHashArray<V>, headers: readonly string[]): (V \| undefined)[]` |
| `Csv.generateRowsAsync` | function | `(options?: (CsvGenerateOptions & { delay?: number \| undefined; }) \| undefined): AsyncGenerator<string, void, undefined>` |

## `documonster/draw`

### Changed signature or type

| Name | Kind | Before | Now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ToSvgOptions.background?` | prop | `readonly string \| undefined` | `readonly string \| DrawRgba01 \| undefined` |
| `downsample` | function | `(source: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>, width: number, height: number, factor: number): Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>` | `(source: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>, width: number, height: number, factor: number): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>` |
| `RasterizeOptions.background?` | prop | `readonly Rgba01 \| undefined` | `readonly string \| DrawRgba01 \| undefined` |
| `RasterizedImage.data` | prop | `readonly Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>` | `readonly Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>` |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#strokeRect` | method | `(x: number, y: number, width: number, height: number, color: string \| undefined, strokeWidth?: number \| undefined): void` | `(x: number, y: number, width: number, height: number, options?: DrawRasterLineOptions \| undefined): void`<br>_(1 unchanged)_ |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#drawPolyline` | method | `(points: RasterPoint[], color: string \| undefined, width?: number \| undefined, dash?: readonly number[] \| undefined, shape?: { join?: "round" \| "miter" \| "bevel…` | `(points: readonly RasterPoint[], options?: DrawRasterStrokeOptions \| undefined): void`<br>`(points: readonly RasterPoint[], color: string \| undefined, width?: number \| undefined, dash?: readonly number[] \| undefined, shape?: Pick<DrawRasterStrokeOptio…` |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#drawLine` | method | `(x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number, color: string \| undefined, width?: number \| undefined): void` | `(x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number, options?: DrawRasterLineOptions \| undefined): void`<br>_(1 unchanged)_ |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#strokeEllipse` | method | `…h?: readonly number[] \| undefined, shape?: { join?: "round" \| "miter" \| "bevel" \| undefined; cap?: "round" \| "square" \| "butt" \| undefined; } \| undefined): void` | `(cx: number, cy: number, rx: number, ry: number, options?: DrawRasterStrokeOptions \| undefined): void`<br>`…: string \| undefined, width?: number \| undefined, dash?: readonly number[] \| undefined, shape?: Pick<DrawRasterStrokeOptions, "join" \| "cap"> \| undefined): void` |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#strokeCircle` | method | `(cx: number, cy: number, r: number, color: string \| undefined, width?: number \| undefined): void` | `(cx: number, cy: number, r: number, options?: DrawRasterLineOptions \| undefined): void`<br>_(1 unchanged)_ |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#fillPolygon` | method | `(points: RasterPoint[], color: string \| undefined): void` | `(points: readonly RasterPoint[], color: string \| undefined): void` |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#fillRings` | method | `(rings: readonly RasterPoint[][], color: string \| undefined, fillRule: "nonzero" \| "evenodd"): void` | `(rings: readonly (readonly RasterPoint[])[], color: string \| undefined, fillRule: "nonzero" \| "evenodd"): void` |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#drawText` | method | `…g \| undefined, anchor: string \| undefined, rotation?: { angle: number; originX: number; originY: number; } \| undefined, style?: RasterTextStyle \| undefined): vo…` | `(x: number, y: number, text: string, options: DrawRasterTextOptions): void`<br>`…ntSize: number, color: string \| undefined, anchor: string \| undefined, rotation?: DrawRasterTextRotation \| undefined, style?: RasterTextStyle \| undefined): void` |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#uncoveredCodePoints` | prop | `readonly Set<number>` | `Set<number>` |

### Newly deprecated (still works; removed in 1.0)

| Name | Kind | Replacement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `cssColour` | function | Use `Draw.parseColor`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `relativeLuminance` | function | Use `Draw.getRelativeLuminance`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `translucent` | function | Use `Draw.withAlpha`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `measureText` | function | Use `Draw.measureText`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `widestText` | function | Use `Draw.measureWidest`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `wrapText` | function | Use `Draw.wrapText`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `renderDrawList` | function | Use `Draw.render`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `renderNode` | function | Use `Draw.Backend.renderNode`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `SvgSurface` | class | Use `Draw.Backend.SvgSurface`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `SvgSurface#markup` | method | Use `format`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `toSvg` | function | Use `Draw.toSvg`, which checks the list and its options. This form keeps writing what it is given, as it always did. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ToSvgOptions` | type | Use `DrawToSvgOptions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createRasterSurface` | function | Use `Draw.Backend.createRasterSurface`, which takes `{ scale }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `downsample` | function | Use `Draw.Backend.downsample`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `normalizeSamples` | function | Use `Draw.Backend.normalizeSamples`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `rasterizeToRgba` | function | Use `Draw.toRgba`, which checks the list and its options and refuses a list with no area. This form keeps its old reading — a 0×0 list rasterises to a 1×1 transparent image. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RasterizeOptions` | type | Use `DrawRasterizeOptions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RasterizedImage` | type | Use `DrawRasterizedImage`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RgbaImage` | type | Use `DrawRgbaImage`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `isSimpleText` | function | Use `Draw.isSimpleText`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `textFeaturesOf` | function | Use `Draw.getTextFeatures`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `BasicRasterCanvas` | class | Use `Draw.Backend.BasicRasterCanvas`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#uncoveredCodePoints` | prop | Use `getUncoveredCodePoints`. This handed out the canvas's own set, so a caller could clear it and change what the render reports; it now returns a copy. Removed in 1.0. |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#textWarnings` | method | Use `getTextWarnings`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RasterPoint` | type | Use `DrawRasterPoint`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `GlyphOutline` | type | Use `DrawGlyphOutline`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RasterFont` | type | Use `DrawRasterFont`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `fontHasGlyph` | function | Use `Draw.Backend.fontHasGlyph`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `parseRasterFont` | function | Use `Draw.parseRasterFont`, which takes the bytes or `{ data, collectionIndex }` and throws for bytes it cannot read (this form returns an empty face, and face 0 for an index past the end of a collection). Removed in 1.0. |
| `RasterFontSource` | type | Use `DrawRasterFontSource`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `IDENTITY` | const | Use `Matrix.IDENTITY`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `apply` | function | Use `Matrix.apply`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `arcToCubics` | function | Use `Draw.Geometry.arcToCubics`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `multiply` | function | Use `Matrix.multiply`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `rotate` | function | Use `Matrix.rotate`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `flattenPath` | function | Use `Draw.Geometry.flattenPath`, which takes `{ scale }` and checks it. This form multiplies by whatever `scale` it is given, as it always did. Removed in 1.0. |
| `rectNode` | function | Use `Draw.Geometry.createRectNode`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `roundedRectToPath` | function | Use `Draw.Geometry.roundedRectToPath`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `rotationOf` | function | Use `Matrix.getRotation`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `scale` | function | Use `Matrix.scale`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `sectorToPath` | function | Use `Draw.Geometry.sectorToPath`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `translate` | function | Use `Matrix.translate`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `uniformScale` | function | Use `Matrix.getUniformScale`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Rgba01` | type | Use `DrawRgba01`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `RasterTextStyle` | type | Use `DrawRasterTextStyle`. Removed in 1.0. |

### Newly deprecated call forms (the name stays; the form described is removed in 1.0)

| Name | Replacement |
| --- | --- |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#strokeRect` | Pass `{ color, width }` as one options object. The positional form is removed in 1.0. |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#drawPolyline` | Pass `{ color, width, dash, join, cap }` as one options object. The positional form is removed in 1.0. |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#drawLine` | Pass `{ color, width }` as one options object, as `strokeRect` takes it. The positional form is removed in 1.0. |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#strokeEllipse` | Pass `{ color, width, dash, join, cap }` as one options object. The positional form is removed in 1.0. |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#strokeCircle` | Pass `{ color, width }` as one options object, as `strokeEllipse` takes it. The positional form is removed in 1.0. |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#drawText` | Pass `{ fontSize, color, anchor, rotation, family, bold, italic }` as one options object. The positional form is removed in 1.0. |

### Added to exports that already existed

| Name                                       | Kind   | Signature      |
| ------------------------------------------ | ------ | -------------- |
| `SvgSurface#format`                        | method | `(): string`   |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#getUncoveredCodePoints` | method | `(): number[]` |
| `BasicRasterCanvas#getTextWarnings`        | method | `(): string[]` |

### Newly deprecated (still works; removed in 1.0)

| Name | Kind | Replacement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `Workbook.getWorksheets` | function | Use `listWorksheets`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.getChartsheets` | function | Use `listChartsheets`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.renameChartsheet` | function | Look the chartsheet up, then rename it: `const cs = Workbook.getChartsheet(wb, nameOrIndex); if (cs) Chartsheet.setName(cs, name);` — this answered `false` when there is no such chartsheet. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.eachSheet` | function | Use `eachWorksheet`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.importSheet` | function | Use `importWorksheet`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.defineCellStyle` | function | Use `addCellStyle`, which returns (a copy of) the stored entry. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.useBuiltinCellStyle` | function | Use `useBuiltInCellStyle`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.createStreamWriter` | function | Use `new Stream.WorkbookWriter(options)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.createStreamReader` | function | Use `new Stream.WorkbookReader(input, options)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.XlsxReadable` | type | Use the flat `XlsxReadable` type. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.XlsxWritable` | type | Use the flat `XlsxWritable` type. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.XlsxStreamOptions` | type | Use the flat `XlsxStreamOptions` type. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.Handle.pivotTables` | prop | Use `Pivot.list`. Removed from the published type in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addMedia` | method | One of the steps `commit` runs to write the package, and only correct there: called on its own it writes the media a second time. Use `commit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#xlsbUnsupported` | prop | Use `unsupported`. Removed in 1.0. What it always returned: the XLSB writer's losses only — empty for an XLSX stream — with a row's loss as `A1: …`. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#xlsbFormulaContext` | method | The sheet writer's view of the workbook while it encodes rows; nothing outside the writer needs it. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#date1904Flag` | method | The sheet writer's view of the workbook while it encodes rows; nothing outside the writer needs it. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#definedNames` | prop | Use `Workbook.getDefinedNames`: `Workbook.getDefinedNames(wb)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#defineCellStyle` | method | Use `Workbook.addCellStyle`, which returns (a copy of) the stored entry. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#packageSink` | method | The sheet writer's view of the workbook while it encodes rows; nothing outside the writer needs it. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#nextId` | prop | The id `addWorksheet` gives the next sheet; nothing outside the writer needs it. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addImage` | method | Use `Image.add`: `Image.add(wb, image)`, then `Image.place(ws, id, range)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#protect` | method | Use `Workbook.protect`: `Workbook.protect(wb, { password, ...flags })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#unprotect` | method | Use `Workbook.unprotect`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addWorksheet` | method | Use `Workbook.addWorksheet`: `Workbook.addWorksheet(wb, name, options)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#getWorksheet` | method | Use `Workbook.getWorksheet`: `Workbook.getWorksheet(wb, id)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addStyles` | method | One of the steps `commit` runs to write the package, and only correct there: called on its own it writes its part a second time. Use `commit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addThemes` | method | One of the steps `commit` runs to write the package, and only correct there: called on its own it writes its part a second time. Use `commit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addOfficeRels` | method | One of the steps `commit` runs to write the package, and only correct there: called on its own it writes its part a second time. Use `commit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addContentTypes` | method | One of the steps `commit` runs to write the package, and only correct there: called on its own it writes its part a second time. Use `commit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addApp` | method | One of the steps `commit` runs to write the package, and only correct there: called on its own it writes its part a second time. Use `commit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addCore` | method | One of the steps `commit` runs to write the package, and only correct there: called on its own it writes its part a second time. Use `commit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addSharedStrings` | method | One of the steps `commit` runs to write the package, and only correct there: called on its own it writes its part a second time. Use `commit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addFeaturePropertyBag` | method | One of the steps `commit` runs to write the package, and only correct there: called on its own it writes its part a second time. Use `commit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addMetadata` | method | One of the steps `commit` runs to write the package, and only correct there: called on its own it writes its part a second time. Use `commit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addWorkbookRels` | method | One of the steps `commit` runs to write the package, and only correct there: called on its own it writes its part a second time. Use `commit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorkbookWriter#addWorkbook` | method | One of the steps `commit` runs to write the package, and only correct there: called on its own it writes its part a second time. Use `commit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.setCellComment` | function | A comment is the cell's note with its author: `Stream.setCellNote(c, note, { author })`, and `removeCellNote` for `undefined`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.rowValues` | function | Use `getRowValues` with `{ base: 1 }` — this reads the row 1-based, by column number. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.rowCell` | function | Use `getRowCell`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#xlsbUnsupported` | prop | Use `unsupported`, which reports both formats and names the sheet. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#stream` | prop | The sheet part's output while the writer encodes it; writing to it directly corrupts the part, and nothing outside the writer needs it. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#mergeCells` | method | Use `Worksheet.merge`: `Worksheet.merge(ws, "A1:B2")`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#addConditionalFormatting` | method | Use `ConditionalFormatting.add`: `ConditionalFormatting.add(ws, cf)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#removeConditionalFormatting` | method | Use `ConditionalFormatting.remove` (one block) or `ConditionalFormatting.clear` (all). Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#addBackgroundImage` | method | Use `Image.setBackground`: `Image.setBackground(ws, imageId)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#getBackgroundImageId` | method | Use `Image.getBackground`, which answers with the id as a string (`"0"`), as it does for a document-model sheet. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#addImage` | method | Use `Image.place`: `Image.place(ws, imageId, range)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#getImages` | method | Use `Image.list`, which lists the placed pictures without the entry an overlay watermark adds. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#addWatermark` | method | Use `Watermark.set`: `Watermark.set(ws, options)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#getWatermark` | method | Use `Watermark.get`, which answers with a copy. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#removeWatermark` | method | Use `Watermark.remove`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#protect` | method | Use `Worksheet.protect`: `Worksheet.protect(ws, { password, ...flags })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#unprotect` | method | Use `Worksheet.unprotect`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Stream.WorksheetWriter#drawing` | prop | The workbook writer's view of the sheet while it writes the package; nothing outside the writer needs it. `getImages()` lists what was added. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.spliceRows` | function | Use `Row.splice`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.spliceColumns` | function | Use `Column.splice`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.insertRow` | function | Use `Row.insert`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.insertRows` | function | Use `Row.insertMany`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.duplicateRow` | function | Use `Row.duplicate`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.destroy` | function | Use `Workbook.removeWorksheet(wb, ws)`, which this does. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.autoFitColumn` | function | Use `Column.autoFit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.autoFitColumns` | function | Use `Column.autoFit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.autoFitRow` | function | Use `Row.autoFit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.autoFitRows` | function | Use `Row.autoFit`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.addConditionalFormatting` | function | Use `ConditionalFormatting.add`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.removeConditionalFormatting` | function | Use `ConditionalFormatting.remove` for an entry, a position or a predicate, and `ConditionalFormatting.clear` for every entry (what calling this without a filter does). Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.dimensions` | function | Use `getDimensions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.columnCount` | function | Use `Column.getCount`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.actualColumnCount` | function | Use `Column.getActualCount`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.rowCount` | function | Use `Row.getCount`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.actualRowCount` | function | Use `Row.getActualCount`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.mergedRegions` | function | Use `getMergedRegions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.setColumns` | function | Use `Column.setDefinitions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.columns` | function | Use `Column.getDefinitions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.columnDefinitions` | function | Use `Column.getDefinitions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.lastColumn` | function | Use `Column.getLast`, which returns the column **number** (or `undefined` for an empty sheet) rather than a column view, and creates no column record. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.lastRow` | function | Use `Row.getLast`, which returns the row **number** rather than the row record. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.panes` | function | Use `getPanes`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.autoFilter` | function | Use `getAutoFilter`, which answers `undefined` rather than `null`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.addRow` | function | Use `Row.add`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.addRows` | function | Use `Row.addMany`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.getRow` | function | Use the `Row` and `Cell` namespaces. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.getRows` | function | Use the `Row` and `Cell` namespaces. Removed in 1.0. |

| `Cell.view` | function | The document model is addressed by position; use `getView` (`Cell.getView(ws, addr)`), or `getValue` and friends. For a streaming handle use `Stream.getCellView`. Removed in 1.0. |

| `Row.eachCell` | function | Use `eachAddress`, which visits by position and takes its options last. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Row.commit` | function | A document-model sheet has nothing to commit — every edit applies as it is made — and a streamed row is committed through its handle: `Stream.commitRow(ws.getRow(row))`. Removed in 1.0. |

| `Chart.clone` | function | Use `copyTo` with the chart's own worksheet — `Chart.copyTo(c, c.worksheet, range)`. It places the same copy at the same default position and returns its handle; the part number this returned is the handle's `chartNumber` (`chartExNumber` for an Office 2016 chart). This name suggested an independent copy, as `Document.clone` returns, but it adds a chart to the sheet. Removed in 1.0. |

| `Table.create` | function | Use `add`. This builds a table bound to `worksheet` and writes its cells, but never registers it on the sheet, so it is not written, not found by `get` or `list`, and its name is not reserved. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Table.model` | function | Use `getModel`, which returns a copy; change the table with `setModel` or the setters. This returns the model the table uses. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Table.commit` | function | Every edit applies as it is made; there is never anything to commit. Removed in 1.0. |

### Newly deprecated call forms (the name stays; the form described is removed in 1.0)

| Name | Replacement |
| --- | --- |
| `Workbook.protect` | Pass the password in the options object: `protect(wb, { password, ...options })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.registerPerson` | Pass the identity in the options object: `registerPerson(wb, name, { userId, providerId })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.read` | Use `read(data, options)`, which returns a new workbook. This form replaces everything `wb` already holds — its sheets, names and properties — with what it reads. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.readWithDiagnostics` | Use `readWithDiagnostics(data, options)`, which reads into a new workbook. This form replaces everything `wb` already holds. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.readFile` | Use `readFile(filename, options)`, which returns a new workbook. This form replaces everything `wb` already holds. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Workbook.readStream` | Use `readStream(stream, options)`, which returns a new workbook. This form replaces everything `wb` already holds. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.fillFormula` | Pass the results and the share type in the options object: `fillFormula(ws, range, formula, { results, shareType })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.protect` | Pass the password in the options object: `protect(ws, { password, ...options })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.freeze` | Pass the counts in the options object: `freeze(ws, { columns, rows })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.split` | Pass the offsets in the options object: `split(ws, { x, y })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Worksheet.getValues` | Without `base` the result is 1-based, while `Row.getValues` and `Column.getValues` default to 0 — so state it: `{ base: 1 }` keeps this result, `{ base: 0 }` indexes as `Row` and `Column` do. Calling without `options` is removed in 1.0. |
| `Cell.getValue` | Pass the cell as `{ row, col }`: `Cell.getValue(ws, { row, col })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Cell.setValue` | Pass the cell as `{ row, col }`: `Cell.setValue(ws, { row, col }, …)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Cell.getDateParts` | Pass the cell as `{ row, col }`: `Cell.getDateParts(ws, { row, col })`. Removed in 1.0. |

| `Table.addRow` | `options.commit` does nothing — every edit applies as it is made. Omit it. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Table.removeRows` | `options.commit` does nothing — every edit applies as it is made. Omit it. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Note.create` | Pass the author in the options object: `create(note, { author })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Anchor.create` | Pass the offset in the options object: `create(ws, address, { offset })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ChartOptionsError` | Pass the field first: `new ExcelChartOptionsError(field, message, options)`. This form leaves `field` empty. Removed in 1.0. |

### Internal fields now marked deprecated

Published by accident; stripped from the types in 1.0, with no replacement. Use the namespace functions instead.

- `Workbook.Handle`: `sourceFilePath?`, `workbookContentType?`, `_worksheets`, `_definedNames`, `_themes?`, `_defaultFont?`, `_cellStyles?`, `_dxfs?`, `_writerExternalLinkCache`, `_tableNames`, `_chartEntries`, `_chartRels`, `_chartStyles`, `_chartColors`, `_chartExStyles`, `_chartExColors`, `_chartExEntries`, `_chartExRels`, `_chartExStructuredEntries`, `_chartsheets`, `_persons`, `_slicerParts`, `_slicerCacheParts`, `_timelineParts`, `_timelineCacheParts`, `_opaqueParts`, `_xlsbPivotCaches?`, `_opaqueContentTypeDefaults`, `_opaqueDrops`, `userFunctions?`
- `Stream.WorkbookWriter`: `sharedStrings`, `styles`, `zipOptions?`, `xlsbTables?`, `compressionLevel`, `media`, `commentRefs`, `dynamicArrayCount`, `zip`, `stream`, `promise`
- `Stream.CellHandle`: `_value`, `_mergeCount`, `_comment?`, `_formulaGhostOwner?`
- `Stream.WorksheetWriter`: `_name`, `_retainedComments`, `tables`, `pivotTables`, `threadedComments`, `formControls`, `_charts`, `_shapes`, `_sparklineGroups`, `_worksheetMcIgnorable`, `_sortStateAutoFilterRef`, `orderNo?`, `sheetNo`, `conditionalFormattings`, `hasComments`, `startedData`, `rId?`
- `Worksheet.Handle`: `_workbook`, `_name`, `_rows`, `_columns`, `_keys`, `_merges`, `_autoFilterCriteria?`, `_sortStateXml?`, `_opaqueRels?`, `_chartsheetPlaceholder?`, `_retainedComments?`, `_xlsbDrawingRelationshipId?`, `_worksheetNamespaceAttributes?`, `_worksheetMcIgnorable?`, `_sortStateAutoFilterRef?`, `_media`, `_shapes`, `_charts`, `_sparklineGroups`, `_headerRowCount?`, `_drawing`, `_watermark`, `_watermarkMedia`, `_commitRow?`
- `Cell.Sheet`: `_workbook`, `_name`, `_rows`, `_columns`, `_keys`, `_merges`, `_autoFilterCriteria?`, `_sortStateXml?`, `_opaqueRels?`, `_chartsheetPlaceholder?`, `_retainedComments?`, `_xlsbDrawingRelationshipId?`, `_worksheetNamespaceAttributes?`, `_worksheetMcIgnorable?`, `_sortStateAutoFilterRef?`, `_media`, `_shapes`, `_charts`, `_sparklineGroups`, `_headerRowCount?`, `_drawing`, `_watermark`, `_watermarkMedia`, `_commitRow?`
- `Row.Sheet`: `_workbook`, `_name`, `_rows`, `_columns`, `_keys`, `_merges`, `_autoFilterCriteria?`, `_sortStateXml?`, `_opaqueRels?`, `_chartsheetPlaceholder?`, `_retainedComments?`, `_xlsbDrawingRelationshipId?`, `_worksheetNamespaceAttributes?`, `_worksheetMcIgnorable?`, `_sortStateAutoFilterRef?`, `_media`, `_shapes`, `_charts`, `_sparklineGroups`, `_headerRowCount?`, `_drawing`, `_watermark`, `_watermarkMedia`, `_commitRow?`
- `Column.Sheet`: `_workbook`, `_name`, `_rows`, `_columns`, `_keys`, `_merges`, `_autoFilterCriteria?`, `_sortStateXml?`, `_opaqueRels?`, `_chartsheetPlaceholder?`, `_retainedComments?`, `_xlsbDrawingRelationshipId?`, `_worksheetNamespaceAttributes?`, `_worksheetMcIgnorable?`, `_sortStateAutoFilterRef?`, `_media`, `_shapes`, `_charts`, `_sparklineGroups`, `_headerRowCount?`, `_drawing`, `_watermark`, `_watermarkMedia`, `_commitRow?`

## `documonster/excel/csv`

### Changed signature or type

| Name | Kind | Before | Now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `createCsvReadStream` | function | `(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: CsvOptions \| undefined): IReadable<Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>> & Readable` | `(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: ExcelCsvWriteOptions \| undefined): IReadable<Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>> & Readable` |
| `readCsv` | function | `(workbook: WorkbookData, input: CsvInput, options?: CsvOptions \| undefined): Promise<WorksheetData>` | `(workbook: WorkbookData, input: ExcelCsvInput, options?: ExcelCsvReadOptions \| undefined): Promise<WorksheetData>` |
| `writeCsv` | function | `(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: CsvOptions \| undefined): string`<br>`(workbook: WorkbookData, stream: IWritable<string \| Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>>, options?: CsvOptions \| undefined): Promise<void>` | `(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: ExcelCsvWriteOptions \| undefined): string`<br>`(workbook: WorkbookData, stream: IWritable<string \| Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>>, options?: ExcelCsvWriteOptions \| undefined): Promise<void>` |
| `writeCsvBuffer` | function | `(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: CsvOptions \| undefined): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>>` | `(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: ExcelCsvWriteOptions \| undefined): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>` |
| `createCsvWriteStream` | function | `(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: CsvOptions \| undefined): IWritable<string \| Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>>` | `(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: ExcelCsvReadOptions \| undefined): IWritable<string \| Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>>` |
| `CsvInput` | type | `type CsvInput = string \| ArrayBuffer \| Uint8Array \| File \| Blob \| IReadable<Uint8Array \| string>;` | `type CsvInput = ExcelCsvInput;` |
| `CsvOptions.delimitersToGuess?` | prop | `string[] \| undefined` | `readonly string[] \| undefined` |
| `readCsvFile` | function | `(workbook: WorkbookData, filename: string, options?: CsvOptions \| undefined): Promise<WorksheetData>` | `(workbook: WorkbookData, filename: string, options?: ExcelCsvReadOptions \| undefined): Promise<WorksheetData>` |
| `writeCsvFile` | function | `(workbook: WorkbookData, filename: string, options?: CsvOptions \| undefined): Promise<void>` | `(workbook: WorkbookData, filename: string, options?: ExcelCsvWriteOptions \| undefined): Promise<void>` |

### Newly deprecated (still works; removed in 1.0)

| Name | Kind | Replacement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `createCsvReadStream` | function | Use `ExcelCsv.toStream`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `readCsv` | function | Use `ExcelCsv.addWorksheet`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `writeCsv` | function | Use `ExcelCsv.format` for a string or `ExcelCsv.writeStream` for a stream. Removed in 1.0. |
| `writeCsvBuffer` | function | Use `ExcelCsv.toBuffer`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createCsvWriteStream` | function | Use `ExcelCsv.createImportStream`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `CsvInput` | type | Use `ExcelCsvInput`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `CsvOptions` | type | Use `ExcelCsvOptions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `CsvOptions.info?` | prop | Has no effect on a workbook: a record's position has nowhere to go in a sheet. Removed in 1.0. |
| `CsvOptions.raw?` | prop | Has no effect on a workbook: a record's raw text has nowhere to go in a sheet. Removed in 1.0. |
| `CsvOptions.skipRecordsWithError?` | prop | Has no effect: a malformed record (a column-count mismatch the `columnMismatch` strategy rejects, or text after a closing quote) is always left out of `rows`, reported in `errors` / `invalidRows` and handed to `onSkip`. It only used to decide whether `onSkip` was called. Removed in 1.0. |
| `readCsvFile` | function | Use `ExcelCsv.readFile` for a new workbook, or `ExcelCsv.addWorksheet` with a file stream to add the sheet to one you have. Removed in 1.0. |
| `writeCsvFile` | function | Use `ExcelCsv.writeFile`. Removed in 1.0. |

### Added to exports that already existed

| Name | Kind | Signature |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `CsvOptions.mapRow?` | prop | `((values: CellValue[], rowNumber: number) => readonly CellValue[]) \| undefined` |

## `documonster/markdown`

### Changed signature or type

| Name | Kind | Before | Now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `MarkdownFormatOptions.columns?` | prop | `(string \| MarkdownColumnConfig)[] \| undefined` | `readonly (string \| MarkdownColumnConfig)[] \| undefined` |
| `MarkdownOptions.columns?` | prop | `(string \| MarkdownColumnConfig)[] \| undefined` | `readonly (string \| MarkdownColumnConfig)[] \| undefined` |
| `Markdown.format` | function | `(headers: string[], rows: unknown[][], options?: MarkdownFormatOptions \| undefined): string` | `(headers: readonly string[], rows: readonly (readonly unknown[])[], options?: MarkdownFormatOptions \| undefined): string` |

### Newly deprecated (still works; removed in 1.0)

| Name | Kind | Replacement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `MarkdownOptions` | type | Worksheet options do not belong to the standalone Markdown module. Use `ExcelMarkdownReadOptions` / `ExcelMarkdownWriteOptions` from `documonster/excel/markdown`, which the `ExcelMarkdown` functions take. Removed in 1.0. |

### Added exports, with every member

| Name | Kind | Signature |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `MarkdownArgumentError` | class | `new (field: string, message: string, options?: BaseErrorOptions \| undefined): MarkdownArgumentError` |
| `MarkdownArgumentError#name` | prop | `string` |
| `MarkdownArgumentError#field` | prop | `readonly string` |
| `MarkdownArgumentError#toJSON` | method | `(): Record<string, unknown>` |
| `MarkdownArgumentError#message` | prop | `string` |
| `MarkdownArgumentError#stack?` | prop | `string \| undefined` |
| `MarkdownArgumentError#cause?` | prop | `unknown` |
| `BaseErrorOptions` | interface |  |
| `BaseErrorOptions.cause?` | prop | `unknown` |

## `documonster/mermaid`

### Changed signature or type

| Name | Kind | Before | Now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `MermaidSyntaxError` | class | `new (message: string): MermaidSyntaxError` | `new (message: string, options?: MermaidParseErrorOptions \| undefined): MermaidParseError` |

### Newly deprecated (still works; removed in 1.0)

| Name | Kind | Replacement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `mermaidToDrawList` | function | Use `Mermaid.toDrawList`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `mermaidToSvg` | function | Use `Mermaid.toSvg`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `MermaidSyntaxError` | class | Use `MermaidParseError` — the same class, under the name every module's parse error has (`XmlParseError`, `MarkdownParseError`, …), so `instanceof` either answers alike. Removed in 1.0. |
| `parseMermaid` | function | Use `Mermaid.parse`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `layoutFlowchart` | function | Use `Mermaid.layoutGraph`, which also lays out state, class, ER, requirement, C4 and architecture diagrams. Removed in 1.0. |
| `LayoutOptions` | type | Use `MermaidLayoutOptions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ThemeOptions` | type | Use `MermaidThemeOptions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `EdgeRoute` | type | Use `MermaidEdgeRoute`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `FlowLayout` | type | Use `MermaidFlowLayout`. Removed in 1.0. |

## `documonster/pdf`

### Became optional (absent instead of `null` / a sentinel)

| Name | Before | Now |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `PdfMetadata.creationDate` | `Date \| null` | `Date \| undefined` |
| `PdfMetadata.modDate` | `Date \| null` | `Date \| undefined` |
| `PdfMetadata.pageSize` | `{ width: number; height: number; } \| null` | `{ width: number; height: number; } \| undefined` |
| `PdfMetadata.xmpXml` | `string \| null` | `string \| undefined` |
| `ExtractedImage.alphaMask` | `Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike> \| null` | `Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> \| undefined` |
| `PdfBookmark.pageIndex` | `number` | `number \| undefined` |

### Changed signature or type

| Name | Kind | Before | Now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Pdf.fromExcel` | function | `(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: ExcelToPdfOptions \| undefined): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>>` | `(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: (Omit<ExcelToPdfOptions, "sheets"> & { readonly sheets?: readonly string[] \| undefined; }) \| undefined): Promise<Uint8Array<A…`<br>`(workbook: WorkbookData, options: Omit<ExcelToPdfOptions, "sheets"> & { readonly sheets: readonly (string \| number)[]; }): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>`<br>`(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: ExcelToPdfOptions \| undefined): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>` |

| `Pdf.fromDocx` | function | `(doc: DocxDocument, options?: DocxToPdfOptions \| undefined): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>>` | `(doc: DocxDocument, options?: DocxToPdfOptions \| undefined): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>` |
| `Pdf.create` | function | `(input: PdfInput, options?: PdfExportOptions \| undefined): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>>` | `(input: PdfInput, options?: PdfExportOptions \| undefined): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>` |
| `Pdf.read` | function | `(data: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>, options?: ReadPdfOptions \| undefined): Promise<ReadPdfResult>` | `(data: ArrayBuffer \| Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike> \| ArrayBufferView<ArrayBufferLike> \| Blob, options?: ReadPdfOptions \| undefined): Promise<ReadPdfResult>` |
| `Pdf.Builder#setEncryption` | method | `(encryption: { userPassword?: string \| undefined; ownerPassword: string; permissions?: Partial<{ print: boolean; modify: boolean; copy: boolean; annotate: boole…` | `(encryption: PdfEncryptionOptions \| undefined): PdfDocumentBuilder` |
| `Pdf.Builder#embedFont` | method | `(fontBytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>, collectionIndex?: number \| undefined): PdfDocumentBuilder` | `(source: PdfFontSource): PdfDocumentBuilder`<br>`(fontBytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>, collectionIndex: number \| undefined): PdfDocumentBuilder` |

### Newly deprecated (still works; removed in 1.0)

| Name | Kind | Replacement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `Pdf.wordChartRenderer` | function | Omit `chartRenderer`: the default draws every chart — classic and ChartEx — with the Excel engine, and this callback draws a classic chart exactly as the default does. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.create` | function | Use `fromTable`. `create` is the name every other namespace uses for a handle constructor, and this returns bytes. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.read` | function | Use `extract`. `read` is the verb that opens a document as its editable object (`Pdf.Editor.read`); this extracts content. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.Builder#textLanguage` | method | Use `PdfDocumentBuilder.setTextLanguage`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.Builder#build` | method | Use `PdfDocumentBuilder.toBuffer`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.Editor#save` | method | Use `PdfEditor.toBuffer` (which keeps the encryption by default; pass `{ encryption: "remove" }` for this method's decrypted copy). Removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.Editor#saveIncremental` | method | Use `toBuffer({ incremental: true })` (whose fallback keeps the encryption by default). Removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.Editor.load` | static | Use `PdfEditor.read`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.verifySignature` | function | Use `Pdf.Signature.verify`, or `Pdf.verifySignatures` to find and check every signature in a file. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.sign` | function | Use `Pdf.Signature.fillPlaceholder(bytes, { certificate, privateKey })` — this only completes a placeholder already in the file. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.buildSignatureDictPlaceholder` | function | Use `Pdf.Signature.buildDictPlaceholder`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.asn1Parse` | function | Use `Pdf.Signature.parseAsn1`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ChartToPdfOptions` | type | Use `PdfFromChartOptions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ExcelToPdfOptions` | type | Use `PdfFromExcelOptions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ExcelToPdfOptions.font?` | prop | Use `PdfDocumentOptions.fonts` (`{ fonts: { default: { regular: bytes } } }`). Removed in 1.0. |
| `DocxToPdfOptions` | type | Use `PdfFromDocxOptions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `DocxToPdfOptions.marginTop?` | prop | Use `margins: { top }`. Removed in 1.0. `margins.top` wins when both are given. |
| `DocxToPdfOptions.marginBottom?` | prop | Use `margins: { bottom }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `DocxToPdfOptions.marginLeft?` | prop | Use `margins: { left }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `DocxToPdfOptions.marginRight?` | prop | Use `margins: { right }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `DocxToPdfOptions.defaultFont?` | prop | Use `defaultFontFamily`, which wins when both are given. Removed in 1.0. |
| `CjkLanguage` | type | Use `PdfCjkLanguage`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `PdfExportOptions.font?` | prop | Use `PdfDocumentOptions.fonts` (`{ fonts: { default: { regular: bytes } } }`). Removed in 1.0. |

| `EditorSaveOptions` | type | Use `PdfEditorToBufferOptions` with `toBuffer()`, or `PdfEditorWriteOptions` for what every write shares. Removed in 1.0. |
| `EditorSignOptions` | type | Use `PdfEditorSignOptions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `PdfContentStream#deferred` | method | The builder's own machinery for text whose encoding is chosen at build time, not part of the drawing API; it leaves the public type in 1.0. Draw with the typed operators or `raw`. |
| `PdfContentStream#preflight` | method | The builder's own preparation step for deferred fragments, not part of the drawing API; it leaves the public type in 1.0. |
| `SignatureVerificationResult` | type | What the deprecated `Pdf.verifySignature` returns; `Pdf.Signature.verify` returns `PdfSignatureCheck`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `CmsSignedData` | type | No public function takes or returns this. Removed in 1.0. |
| `SignOptions` | type | No public function takes this; signing options are `PdfSignOptions`(`Pdf.Signature.fillPlaceholder`) and `PdfSignatureOptions` (`sign`). Removed in 1.0. |
| `Asn1Node` | type | Use `PdfAsn1Node`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createPdfDrawSurface` | function | Use `Pdf.createDrawSurface(page, rect, { scale })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `DrawSurfaceRect` | type | Use `PdfDrawSurfaceRect`. Removed in 1.0. |

### Newly deprecated call forms (the name stays; the form described is removed in 1.0)

| Name | Replacement |
| --- | --- |
| `Pdf.fromExcel` | Pass sheet names. Numeric positions are deprecated (removed in 1.0): they are 1-based here but 0-based in `WordExcel.toDocx` and sheet ids in `Workbook.getWorksheet`. The form that takes numeric positions in `sheets`; it works exactly as before until it is removed. |
| `Pdf.Builder#embedFont` | Pass `{ data: fontBytes, collectionIndex }` as one `PdfFontSource`. The positional `collectionIndex` is removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.Editor#splitPages` | Pass `{ pageIndices }` as one options object. The positional form is removed in 1.0. It skips an index out of range or naming a removed page, as it always has, and throws `PdfStructureError` for one in range that is not an integer. |
| `Pdf.Editor#embedFont` | Pass `{ data: fontBytes, collectionIndex }` as one `PdfFontSource`. The positional `collectionIndex` is removed in 1.0. |
| `Pdf.Editor#copyPagesFrom` | Pass `{ pageIndices, password }` as one options object. The positional form is removed in 1.0. |
| `PdfLimitExceededError` | Pass the limit and its value first: `new PdfLimitExceededError(limit, allowed, message, options)`. This form leaves `limit` and `allowed` undefined. Removed in 1.0. |

### Newly deprecated (still works; removed in 1.0)

| Name | Kind | Replacement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `Collector` | class | Use `Streams.Collector` (`new Streams.Collector(options)`). Removed in 1.0. |
| `createCollector` | function | Use `new Streams.Collector(options)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `PullStream` | class | Use `Streams.PullStream` (`new Streams.PullStream(options)`). Removed in 1.0. |
| `BufferedStream` | class | Use `Streams.BufferedStream` (`new Streams.BufferedStream(options)`). Removed in 1.0. |
| `BufferedStream#toUint8Array` | method | Use `readAll`, which does the same: despite the `to` name this consumes the buffered data. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createStringChunk` | function | Use `Streams.createStringChunk`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createByteChunk` | function | Use `Streams.createByteChunk`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createReadable` | function | Use `Streams.createReadable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createReadableFromAsyncIterable` | function | Use `Streams.createReadableFromAsyncIterable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createReadableFromArray` | function | Use `Streams.createReadableFromArray`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createWritable` | function | Use `Streams.createWritable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createTransform` | function | Use `Streams.createTransform`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createPassThrough` | function | Use `Streams.createPassThrough`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createPullStream` | function | Use `new Streams.PullStream(options)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createBufferedStream` | function | Use `new Streams.BufferedStream(options)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createDuplex` | function | Use `Streams.createDuplex`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createReadableFromGenerator` | function | Use `Streams.createReadableFromGenerator`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createReadableFromPromise` | function | Use `Streams.createReadableFromPromise`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createEmptyReadable` | function | Use `Streams.createEmptyReadable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `createNullWritable` | function | Use `Streams.createNullWritable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `filter` | function | Use `Streams.filter`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `fromBytes` | function | Use `Streams.fromBytes`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `fromJSON` | function | Use `Streams.fromJson`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `fromString` | function | Use `Streams.fromString`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `transform` | function | Use `Streams.createTransform`, which this duplicated. Removed in 1.0. |
| `finishedAll` | function | Use `Streams.waitForFinished`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `streamToPromise` | function | Use `finished`, which this duplicated. Removed in 1.0. |
| `streamToUint8Array` | function | Use `Streams.bytes`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `streamToBuffer` | function | Use `Streams.bytes`, which this duplicated: both resolve to a `Uint8Array`, not a `Buffer`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `streamToString` | function | Use `Streams.text`; pass the encoding as `{ encoding }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `drainStream` | function | Use `Streams.discard`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `copyStream` | function | Use `pipeline`, which this duplicated. Removed in 1.0. |
| `isTransform` | function | Use `Streams.isTransform`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `isDuplex` | function | Use `Streams.isDuplex`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `isStream` | function | Use `Streams.isStream`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `readableFromWeb` | function | Use `Streams.fromWebReadable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `readableToWeb` | function | Use `Streams.toWebReadable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `toWritable` | function | Use `Streams.toWritable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `writableFromWeb` | function | Use `Streams.fromWebWritable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `writableToWeb` | function | Use `Streams.toWebWritable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `IBufferedStream.toUint8Array` | method | The class's `readAll()` is the same read under a name that says it consumes. Removed in 1.0. |
| `DataChunk` | type | Use `StreamDataChunk`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `EventListener` | type | Use `StreamEventListener`; the bare name shadows the DOM's global `EventListener`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `promisify` | function | Use `Streams.LowLevel.fromCallback`: `promisify` read as `node:util`'s, which wraps a function rather than running one. Removed in 1.0. |
| `ChunkedBuilder` | class | Use `Streams.ChunkedBuilder`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `TransactionalChunkedBuilder` | class | Use `Streams.TransactionalChunkedBuilder`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `bytes` | function | Use `Streams.bytes`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `collect` | function | Use `Streams.collect`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `isReadableStreamLike` | function | Use `Streams.isReadableStream`, which this duplicated. Removed in 1.0. |
| `json` | function | Use `Streams.json`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `readableStreamToAsyncIterable` | function | Use `Streams.readableStreamToAsyncIterable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `text` | function | Use `Streams.text`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `StreamTypeError` | class | Use `StreamArgumentError`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `UnsupportedStreamTypeError` | class | Use `StreamUnsupportedTypeError`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `isReadableStream` | function | Use `Streams.isReadableStream`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `isWritableStream` | function | Use `Streams.isWritableStream`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `isAsyncIterable` | function | Use `Streams.isAsyncIterable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `isTransformStream` | function | Use `Streams.isTransformStream`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `onceEvent` | function | Use `Streams.LowLevel.onceEvent`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `eventedReadableToAsyncIterableNoDestroy` | function | Use `Streams.LowLevel.eventsToAsyncIterableNoDestroy`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `SinkAdapter` | class | Use `Streams.LowLevel.SinkAdapter`. Removed in 1.0. |

### Newly deprecated call forms (the name stays; the form described is removed in 1.0)

| Name                   | Replacement                                                        |
| ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `PullStream#pull`      | Use `pullUntil`, which takes `{ includePattern }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `PullStream#pullUntil` | Pass `{ includePattern }`. Removed in 1.0.                         |

### Newly deprecated (still works; removed in 1.0)

| Name | Kind | Replacement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `DocxOptions` | interface | No function takes this type; use `DocxWriteOptions` (the options of `Document.toBuffer`), which has `compressionLevel` too. Removed in 1.0. |
| `DocxLimitExceededError#maximum` | prop | Use `allowed`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Document.insertContentAt` | function | Use `insertContent`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Document.addParagraphElement` | function | Use `addParagraph`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Document.addTableElement` | function | Use `addTable`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Document.nextBookmarkId` | function | Use `allocateBookmarkId`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Document.build` | function | A document needs no build step: pass the document itself to `Document.toBuffer`, `Layout.*`, `WordHtml.fromDocx` or `Pdf.fromDocx`, which apply the same write-time defaults. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Document.DocumentHandle` | type | Use `DocxDocument`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `DocumentHandle` | type | Use `DocxDocument`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.checkBox` | function | Use `checkbox`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.mathSuperScript` | function | Use `mathSuperscript`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.mathSubScript` | function | Use `mathSubscript`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.mathSubSuperScript` | function | Use `mathSubSuperscript`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.mathPreSubSuperScript` | function | Use `mathPreSubSuperscript`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.drawingShape` | function | Use `shape`, which builds the same shape and more: `fillColor` → `fill: { type: "solid", color }`, `noFill` → `fill: { type: "none" }`, `outlineColor` / `outlineWidth` / `noOutline` → `outline: { color, width, noLine }`, `textContent` → `textBody: { paragraphs }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.createShape` | function | Use `shape`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.createRect` | function | Use `rect`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.createRoundRect` | function | Use `roundRect`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.createEllipse` | function | Use `ellipse`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.createLine` | function | Use `line`. Removed in 1.0. |

| `Build.createStar` | function | Use `star`. Removed in 1.0. |

### Newly deprecated call forms (the name stays; the form described is removed in 1.0)

| Name | Replacement |
| --- | --- |
| `Document.addImage` | Pass the format and size in the options: `Document.addImage(doc, data, { type, width, height })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Document.addFloatingImage` | Pass the format and size in the options: `Document.addFloatingImage(doc, data, { type, width, height, … })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.underline` | Pass the style in the properties: `underline(content, { ...properties, underline: style })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.field` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.pageNumberField` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.totalPagesField` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.sectionPagesField` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.sectionField` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.dateField` | Pass an options object: `dateField({ format, cachedValue })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.timeField` | Pass an options object: `timeField({ format, cachedValue })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.authorField` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.titleField` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.subjectField` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.keywordsField` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.fileSizeField` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.quoteField` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.ifField` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.includePictureField` | Pass `{ cachedValue }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.mathFraction` | Pass the type in options: `mathFraction(numerator, denominator, { fractionType })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.mathSum` | Pass the limits in options: `mathSum(content, { sub, sup })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.mathIntegral` | Pass the limits in options: `mathIntegral(content, { sub, sup })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.mathProduct` | Pass the limits in options: `mathProduct(content, { sub, sup })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.mathNary` | Pass the limits in options: `mathNary(char, content, { sub, sup })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.mathLimit` | Pass the type in options: `mathLimit(base, limit, { limitType })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.mathAccent` | Pass the character in options: `mathAccent(content, { char })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.mathBar` | Pass the position in options: `mathBar(content, { position })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.border` | Pass an options object: `border({ style, size, color })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.gridBorders` | Pass an options object: `gridBorders({ size, color })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Build.table` | Pass the widths in the options: `table(rows, { ...properties, columnWidths })`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Font.hyphenateWord` | Pass the hyphen character as `{ hyphenChar }`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `Font.hyphenateText` | Pass the hyphen character as `{ hyphenChar }`. Removed in 1.0. |

### Newly deprecated (still works; removed in 1.0)

| Name | Kind | Replacement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `isEncryptedDocx` | function | Use `WordCrypto.isEncrypted`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `verifyPassword` | function | Use `WordCrypto.Agile.verifyPassword`, which takes `(info, password)` instead of `(password, info)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `decryptPackage` | function | Use `WordCrypto.Agile.decryptPackage`, which takes the cap in its options (`{ maxDecryptedSize }`). Removed in 1.0. |
| `decryptDocx` | function | Use `WordCrypto.decrypt`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `encryptDocx` | function | Use `WordCrypto.encrypt`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `parseEncryptionInfoXml` | function | Use `WordCrypto.Agile.parseEncryptionInfo`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `deriveEncryptionKey` | function | Use `WordCrypto.Agile.deriveKey`, which takes `(info, password, blockKey)` instead of `(password, info, blockKey)`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `AGILE_BLOCK_KEYS` | const | Use `WordCrypto.Agile.BLOCK_KEYS`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `AgileEncryptionInfo` | type | Use `DocxAgileEncryptionInfo`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `EncryptOptions` | type | Use `DocxEncryptOptions`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `readCfb` | function | Use `WordCrypto.Cfb.read`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `writeCfb` | function | Use `WordCrypto.Cfb.write`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `CfbEntry` | type | Use `DocxCfbEntry`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `hasDigitalSignatures` | function | Use `WordCrypto.hasSignatures`, which takes the document or the package's part names (`Package.listParts(bytes)`). Removed in 1.0. |
| `parseSignatureXml` | function | Use `WordCrypto.parseSignature`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `extractSignatures` | function | Use `WordCrypto.listSignatures`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `isWellFormedSignature` | function | Use `WordCrypto.isWellFormedSignature`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `DigitalSignatureInfo` | type | Use `DocxDigitalSignatureInfo`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `DigitalSignatureInfo.signer?` | prop | Use `claimedSigner`, which holds the same value: the name is what the signature claims, not a verified signer. Removed in 1.0. |
| `deobfuscateFont` | function | Use `Font.deobfuscate` from `documonster/word`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `obfuscateFont` | function | Use `Font.obfuscate` from `documonster/word`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `generateFontKey` | function | Use `Font.generateObfuscationKey` from `documonster/word`. Removed in 1.0. |

## `documonster/word/excel`

### Changed signature or type

| Name | Kind | Before | Now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `excelToDocx` | function | `(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: ExcelToDocxOptions \| undefined): DocxDocument` | `(workbook: WorkbookData, options?: (Omit<ExcelToDocxOptions, "sheets"> & { readonly sheets?: readonly string[] \| undefined; }) \| undefined): DocxDocument`<br>`(workbook: WorkbookData, options: Omit<ExcelToDocxOptions, "sheets"> & { readonly sheets: readonly (string \| number)[]; }): DocxDocument`<br>_(1 unchanged)_ |
| `renderWordChartSvg` | function | `(chart: Chart): string` | `(chart: Chart, options?: DocxChartSvgOptions \| undefined): string` |
| `generateChartEmbeddedXlsx` | function | `(series: readonly { name: string; categories: readonly string[]; values: readonly number[]; }[]): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>>` | `(series: readonly { name: string; categories: readonly string[]; values: readonly number[]; }[]): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>` |

### Newly deprecated (still works; removed in 1.0)

| Name                        | Kind     | Replacement                                    |
| --------------------------- | -------- | ---------------------------------------------- |
| `extractTablesToExcel`      | function | Use `WordExcel.extractTables`. Removed in 1.0. |
| `renderWordChartSvg`        | function | Use `WordExcel.toChartSvg`. Removed in 1.0.    |
| `buildWordChartExXml`       | function | Use `WordExcel.toChartExXml`. Removed in 1.0.  |
| `wordChartToChartModel`     | function | Use `WordExcel.toChartModel`. Removed in 1.0.  |
| `generateChartEmbeddedXlsx` | function | Use `WordExcel.toChartXlsx`. Removed in 1.0.   |
| `ExcelToDocxOptions`        | type     | Use `DocxFromExcelOptions`. Removed in 1.0.    |
| `WordChartExOptions`        | type     | Use `DocxChartExOptions`. Removed in 1.0.      |

### Newly deprecated call forms (the name stays; the form described is removed in 1.0)

| Name | Replacement |
| --- | --- |
| `excelToDocx` | Pass sheet names. Numeric positions are deprecated (removed in 1.0): they are 0-based here but 1-based in `Pdf.fromExcel` and sheet ids in `Workbook.getWorksheet`. The form that takes numeric positions in `sheets`; it works exactly as before until it is removed. |

## `@documonster/mcp`

### TypeScript entry

| Name                          | Change            | Signature                      |
| ----------------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------ |
| `PdfFontRef`                  | added (interface) |                                |
| `PdfFontRef.path`             | added (prop)      | `readonly string`              |
| `PdfFontRef.collectionIndex?` | added (prop)      | `readonly number \| undefined` |

### Tools

22 tools in the groups `core`, `excel`, `word`, `pdf`, `forms`, `archive`, `diagram`. No tool was added. No tool was removed. Input schemas were compared as JSON Schema.

| Tool | Change |
| --- | --- |
| `pdf_edit` | schema `/properties/ops/items/oneOf/7/properties/formFields`: added; description changed |

Every other tool is identical. Behaviour changes are described in [MIGRATION.md](../MIGRATION.md#documonstermcp).
